ib\+\_\+alloc\+\_\+pd -\/ Allocates an unused protection domain. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} device on which to allocate the protection domain. @flags\+: protection domain flags

A protection domain object provides an association between Q\+Ps, shared receive queues, address handles, memory regions, and memory windows.

Every PD has a local\+\_\+dma\+\_\+lkey which can be used as the lkey value for local memory operations. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a970eadab07227ce5b4af19e83f676251}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a970eadab07227ce5b4af19e83f676251}}

rdma\+\_\+device\+\_\+to\+\_\+drv\+\_\+device -\/ Helper macro to reach back to driver\textquotesingle{}s \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__device}{ib\+\_\+device}} holder structure from device pointer.

N\+O\+TE\+: New drivers should not make use of this A\+PI; This A\+PI is only for existing drivers who have exposed sysfs entries using ops-\/$>$device\+\_\+group. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a7c212c5131a5e7f6bb93b60f72f6d286}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a7c212c5131a5e7f6bb93b60f72f6d286}}

rdma\+\_\+for\+\_\+each\+\_\+block -\/ iterate over contiguous memory blocks of the sg list @sglist\+: sglist to iterate over @biter\+: block iterator holding the memory block @nents\+: maximum number of sg entries to iterate over @pgsz\+: best HW supported page size to use

Callers may use \mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_a40798dbe74aadef5632857d08b143702}{rdma\+\_\+block\+\_\+iter\+\_\+dma\+\_\+address()}} to get each blocks aligned D\+MA address. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_af339ed7c06f36e6da8d3d1e867dfc5c7}\label{ib__verbs_8h_af339ed7c06f36e6da8d3d1e867dfc5c7}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_a131d3876e11fda7f0aca3629bab8211a}{ib\+\_\+mr\+\_\+type}}}

enum ib\+\_\+mr\+\_\+type -\/ memory region type @\+I\+B\+\_\+\+M\+R\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+M\+E\+M\+\_\+\+R\+EG\+: memory region that is used for normal registration @\+I\+B\+\_\+\+M\+R\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+S\+G\+\_\+\+G\+A\+PS\+: memory region that is capable to register any arbitrary sg lists (without the normal mr constraints -\/ see ib\+\_\+map\+\_\+mr\+\_\+sg) @\+I\+B\+\_\+\+M\+R\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+DM\+: memory region that is used for device memory registration @\+I\+B\+\_\+\+M\+R\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+U\+S\+ER\+: memory region that is used for the user-\/space application @\+I\+B\+\_\+\+M\+R\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+D\+MA\+: memory region that is used for D\+MA operations without address translations (VA=PA) @\+I\+B\+\_\+\+M\+R\+\_\+\+T\+Y\+P\+E\+\_\+\+I\+N\+T\+E\+G\+R\+I\+TY\+: memory region that is used for data integrity operations \begin{DoxyEnumFields}{Enumerator}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__cq}{ib\+\_\+cq}}$\ast$ \+\_\+\+\_\+ib\+\_\+create\+\_\+cq (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__device}{ib\+\_\+device}} $\ast$}]{device,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_a10b2e4734fadda08a0b10073005f4731}{ib\+\_\+comp\+\_\+handler}}}]{comp\+\_\+handler,  }\item[{void($\ast$)(struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__event}{ib\+\_\+event}} $\ast$, void $\ast$)}]{event\+\_\+handler,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{cq\+\_\+context,  }\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__cq__init__attr}{ib\+\_\+cq\+\_\+init\+\_\+attr}} $\ast$}]{cq\+\_\+attr,  }\item[{const char $\ast$}]{caller }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ib\+\_\+create\+\_\+cq -\/ Creates a CQ on the specified device. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} device on which to create the CQ. @comp\+\_\+handler\+: A user-\/specified callback that is invoked when a completion event occurs on the CQ. @event\+\_\+handler\+: A user-\/specified callback that is invoked when an asynchronous event not associated with a completion occurs on the CQ. @cq\+\_\+context\+: Context associated with the CQ returned to the user via the associated completion and event handlers. @cq\+\_\+attr\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} attributes the CQ should be created upon.

Users can examine the cq structure to determine the actual CQ size. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a759ea490023afaff13d9f6875b01aa28}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a759ea490023afaff13d9f6875b01aa28}}

ib\+\_\+attach\+\_\+mcast -\/ Attaches the specified QP to a multicast group. @qp\+: QP to attach to the multicast group. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} QP must be type I\+B\+\_\+\+Q\+P\+T\+\_\+\+UD. @gid\+: Multicast group G\+ID. @lid\+: Multicast group L\+ID in host byte order.

In order to send and receive multicast packets, subnet administration must have created the multicast group and configured the fabric appropriately. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} port associated with the specified QP must also be a member of the multicast group. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a6bf7c0a2b246dc3ee523d8671354df19}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a6bf7c0a2b246dc3ee523d8671354df19}}

ib\+\_\+check\+\_\+mr\+\_\+status\+: lightweight check of MR status. This routine may provide status checks on a selected \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__mr}{ib\+\_\+mr}}. first use is for signature status check.

@mr\+: A memory region. @check\+\_\+mask\+: Bitmask of which checks to perform from ib\+\_\+mr\+\_\+status\+\_\+check enumeration. @mr\+\_\+status\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} container of relevant status checks. failed checks will be indicated in the status bitmask and the relevant info shall be in the error item. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a0153b9742a6e62741bef5e9f150cf669}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a0153b9742a6e62741bef5e9f150cf669}}

ib\+\_\+create\+\_\+ah\+\_\+from\+\_\+wc -\/ Creates an address handle associated with the sender of the specified work completion. @pd\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} protection domain associated with the address handle. @wc\+: Work completion information associated with a received message. @grh\+: References the received global route header. This parameter is ignored unless the work completion indicates that the G\+RH is valid. @port\+\_\+num\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} outbound port number to associate with the address.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} address handle is used to reference a local or global destination in all UD QP post sends. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a8f4866f27d8008c629e95ed3ee1e6cc0}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a8f4866f27d8008c629e95ed3ee1e6cc0}}

ib\+\_\+device\+\_\+try\+\_\+get\+: Hold a registration lock device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} device to lock

A device under an active registration lock cannot become unregistered. It is only possible to obtain a registration lock on a device that is fully registered, otherwise this function returns false.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} registration lock is only necessary for actions which require the device to still be registered. Uses that only require the device pointer to be valid should use get\+\_\+device(\&ibdev-\/$>$dev) to hold the memory. Here is the call graph for this function\+:

ib\+\_\+get\+\_\+client\+\_\+data -\/ Get IB client context @device\+:\mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to get context for @client\+:Client to get context for

\mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_a3d31ce746be68de14fd728602dd4548c}{ib\+\_\+get\+\_\+client\+\_\+data()}} returns the client context data set with \mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_afec2df44194e11210b1c4e9f1cc6ad5a}{ib\+\_\+set\+\_\+client\+\_\+data()}}. This can only be called while the client is registered to the device, once the \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__client}{ib\+\_\+client}} \mbox{\hyperlink{btree-type_8h_af9459a02577bdb33e1ea04f4e7e72126}{remove()}} callback returns this cannot be called. ib\+\_\+inc\+\_\+rkey -\/ increments the key portion of the given rkey. Can be used for calculating a new rkey for type 2 memory windows. @rkey -\/ the rkey to increment. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a5b16adbdafa41415a05aabbebc19b945}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a5b16adbdafa41415a05aabbebc19b945}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ib\_init\_ah\_attr\_from\_wc()}{ib\_init\_ah\_attr\_from\_wc()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} ib\+\_\+init\+\_\+ah\+\_\+attr\+\_\+from\+\_\+wc (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__device}{ib\+\_\+device}} $\ast$}]{device,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{port\+\_\+num,  }\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__wc}{ib\+\_\+wc}} $\ast$}]{wc,  }\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__grh}{ib\+\_\+grh}} $\ast$}]{grh,  }\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structrdma__ah__attr}{rdma\+\_\+ah\+\_\+attr}} $\ast$}]{ah\+\_\+attr }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

ib\+\_\+init\+\_\+ah\+\_\+attr\+\_\+from\+\_\+wc -\/ Initializes address handle attributes from a work completion. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} on which the received message arrived. @port\+\_\+num\+: Port on which the received message arrived. @wc\+: Work completion associated with the received message. @grh\+: References the received global route header. This parameter is ignored unless the work completion indicates that the G\+RH is valid. @ah\+\_\+attr\+: Returned attributes that can be used when creating an address handle for replying to the message. When \mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_a5b16adbdafa41415a05aabbebc19b945}{ib\+\_\+init\+\_\+ah\+\_\+attr\+\_\+from\+\_\+wc()}} returns success, (a) for IB link layer it optionally \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} a reference to S\+G\+ID attribute when G\+RH is present for IB link layer. (b) for Ro\+CE link layer it \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} a reference to S\+G\+ID attribute. User must invoke rdma\+\_\+cleanup\+\_\+ah\+\_\+attr\+\_\+gid\+\_\+attr() to release reference to S\+G\+ID attributes which are initialized using \mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_a5b16adbdafa41415a05aabbebc19b945}{ib\+\_\+init\+\_\+ah\+\_\+attr\+\_\+from\+\_\+wc()}}. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a1b77ee3f33d1de3a71b90074aca28fe3}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a1b77ee3f33d1de3a71b90074aca28fe3}}

ib\+\_\+modify\+\_\+qp -\/ Modifies the attributes for the specified QP and then transitions the QP to the given state. @qp\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} QP to modify. @qp\+\_\+attr\+: On input, specifies the QP attributes to modify. On output, the current values of selected QP attributes are returned. @qp\+\_\+attr\+\_\+mask\+: A bit-\/mask used to specify which attributes of the QP are being modified. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_ac161147bb631075b9eff67ae04d8ca04}\label{ib__verbs_8h_ac161147bb631075b9eff67ae04d8ca04}}

ib\+\_\+modify\+\_\+qp\+\_\+is\+\_\+ok -\/ Check that the supplied attribute mask \mbox{\hyperlink{protocolcontains-p}{contains}} all required attributes and no attributes not allowed for the given QP state transition. @cur\+\_\+state\+: Current QP state @next\+\_\+state\+: Next QP state @type\+: QP type @mask\+: Mask of supplied QP attributes

This function is a helper function that a low-\/level driver\textquotesingle{}s modify\+\_\+qp method can use to validate the consumer\textquotesingle{}s input. It checks that cur\+\_\+state and next\+\_\+state are valid QP states, that a transition from cur\+\_\+state to next\+\_\+state is allowed by the IB spec, and that the attribute mask supplied is allowed for the transition. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a63f26a812745567e5b2677e1497c26fa}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a63f26a812745567e5b2677e1497c26fa}}

ib\+\_\+modify\+\_\+qp\+\_\+with\+\_\+udata -\/ Modifies the attributes for the specified QP. @qp\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} QP to modify. @attr\+: On input, specifies the QP attributes to modify. On output, the current values of selected QP attributes are returned. @attr\+\_\+mask\+: A bit-\/mask used to specify which attributes of the QP are being modified. @udata\+: pointer to user\textquotesingle{}s input output buffer information are being modified. It returns 0 on success and returns appropriate error code on error. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a8fb4574300201a3ac1e251d076b89ecb}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a8fb4574300201a3ac1e251d076b89ecb}}

ib\+\_\+modify\+\_\+srq -\/ Modifies the attributes for the specified S\+RQ. @srq\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} S\+RQ to modify. @srq\+\_\+attr\+: On input, specifies the S\+RQ attributes to modify. On output, the current values of selected S\+RQ attributes are returned. @srq\+\_\+attr\+\_\+mask\+: A bit-\/mask used to specify which attributes of the S\+RQ are being modified.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} mask may contain I\+B\+\_\+\+S\+R\+Q\+\_\+\+M\+A\+X\+\_\+\+WR to resize the S\+RQ and/or I\+B\+\_\+\+S\+R\+Q\+\_\+\+L\+I\+M\+IT to set the S\+RQ\textquotesingle{}s limit and request notification when the number of receives queued drops below the limit. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_ae40474fa736144084ae3beecb7a4cf35}\label{ib__verbs_8h_ae40474fa736144084ae3beecb7a4cf35}}

ib\+\_\+poll\+\_\+cq -\/ poll a CQ for \mbox{\hyperlink{structcompletion}{completion(s)}} @cq\+:the CQ being polled @num\+\_\+entries\+:maximum number of completions to return @wc\+:array of at least @num\+\_\+entries \&struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__wc}{ib\+\_\+wc}} where completions will be returned

Poll a CQ for (possibly multiple) completions. If the return value is $<$ 0, an error occurred. If the return value is $>$= 0, it is the number of completions returned. If the return value is non-\/negative and $<$ num\+\_\+entries, then the CQ was emptied. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_acf1131c37d64f75fdd3e7e44c4562b3c}\label{ib__verbs_8h_acf1131c37d64f75fdd3e7e44c4562b3c}}

While I\+BA Vol. 1 section 11.\+4.\+1.\+1 specifies that if an immediate error is returned, the QP state shall not be affected, \mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_a3e151bb991aad02857ef3ab0f0b57050}{ib\+\_\+post\+\_\+send()}} will return an immediate error after queueing any earlier work requests in the list. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_aabc2eb0352170e2738ceb436be60683d}\label{ib__verbs_8h_aabc2eb0352170e2738ceb436be60683d}}

ib\+\_\+query\+\_\+qp -\/ Returns the attribute list and current values for the specified QP. @qp\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} QP to query. @qp\+\_\+attr\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} attributes of the specified QP. @qp\+\_\+attr\+\_\+mask\+: A bit-\/mask used to select specific attributes to query. @qp\+\_\+init\+\_\+attr\+: Additional attributes of the selected QP.

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} qp\+\_\+attr\+\_\+mask may be used to limit the query to gathering only the selected attributes. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a7a541c1e94541b7dbf8c194ca166e01a}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a7a541c1e94541b7dbf8c194ca166e01a}}

ib\+\_\+rate\+\_\+to\+\_\+mbps -\/ Convert the IB rate enum to Mbps. For example, I\+B\+\_\+\+R\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+2\+\_\+5\+\_\+\+G\+B\+PS will be converted to 2500. @rate\+: rate to convert. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a3a4e3e9dd9ff8b50f86d8b785b6d7cad}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a3a4e3e9dd9ff8b50f86d8b785b6d7cad}}

ib\+\_\+rate\+\_\+to\+\_\+mult -\/ Convert the IB rate enum to a multiple of the base rate of 2.\+5 Gbit/sec. For example, I\+B\+\_\+\+R\+A\+T\+E\+\_\+5\+\_\+\+G\+B\+PS will be converted to 2, since 5 Gbit/sec is 2 $\ast$ 2.\+5 Gbit/sec. @rate\+: rate to convert. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a700065615178a670f7d7255ddcaff8eb}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a700065615178a670f7d7255ddcaff8eb}}

\mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a99961f471e92abe4f188b6de1204e97c}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a99961f471e92abe4f188b6de1204e97c}} 
\index{ib\_verbs.h@{ib\_verbs.h}!ib\_req\_notify\_cq@{ib\_req\_notify\_cq}}
\index{ib\_req\_notify\_cq@{ib\_req\_notify\_cq}!ib\_verbs.h@{ib\_verbs.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ib\_req\_notify\_cq()}{ib\_req\_notify\_cq()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} ib\+\_\+req\+\_\+notify\+\_\+cq (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__cq}{ib\+\_\+cq}} $\ast$}]{cq,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{ib__verbs_8h_ada1c6741d133b0a4b65c3ab35d7b28cc}{ib\+\_\+cq\+\_\+notify\+\_\+flags}}}]{flags }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

ib\+\_\+req\+\_\+notify\+\_\+cq -\/ Request completion notification on a CQ. @cq\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} CQ to generate an event for. @flags\+: Must contain exactly one of I\+B\+\_\+\+C\+Q\+\_\+\+S\+O\+L\+I\+C\+I\+T\+ED or I\+B\+\_\+\+C\+Q\+\_\+\+N\+E\+X\+T\+\_\+\+C\+O\+MP to request an event on the next solicited event or next work completion at any type, respectively. I\+B\+\_\+\+C\+Q\+\_\+\+R\+E\+P\+O\+R\+T\+\_\+\+M\+I\+S\+S\+E\+D\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+TS may also be $\vert$ed in to request a hint about missed events, as described below.

Return Value\+: $<$ 0 means an error occurred while requesting notification == 0 means notification was requested successfully, and if I\+B\+\_\+\+C\+Q\+\_\+\+R\+E\+P\+O\+R\+T\+\_\+\+M\+I\+S\+S\+E\+D\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+TS was passed in, then no events were missed and it is safe to wait for another event. In this case is it guaranteed that any work completions added to the CQ since the last CQ poll will trigger a completion notification event. \begin{quote}
0 is only returned if I\+B\+\_\+\+C\+Q\+\_\+\+R\+E\+P\+O\+R\+T\+\_\+\+M\+I\+S\+S\+E\+D\+\_\+\+E\+V\+E\+N\+TS was passed \end{quote}
in. It means that the consumer must poll the CQ again to make sure it is empty to avoid missing an event because of a race between requesting notification and an entry being added to the CQ. This return value means it is possible (but not guaranteed) that a work completion has been added to the CQ since the last poll without triggering a completion notification event. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a3aa8e1fbb8c3da439b0ee538de24096d}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a3aa8e1fbb8c3da439b0ee538de24096d}}

ib\+\_\+resize\+\_\+cq -\/ Modifies the capacity of the CQ. @cq\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} CQ to resize. @cqe\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} minimum size of the CQ.

Users can examine the cq structure to determine the actual CQ size. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_afec2df44194e11210b1c4e9f1cc6ad5a}\label{ib__verbs_8h_afec2df44194e11210b1c4e9f1cc6ad5a}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}} rdma\+\_\+calc\+\_\+flow\+\_\+label (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{lqpn,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{rqpn }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

rdma\+\_\+calc\+\_\+flow\+\_\+label -\/ generate a R\+D\+MA symmetric flow label value based on local and remote qpn values

This function folded the multiplication results of two qpns, 24 bit each, fields, and converts it to a 20 bit results.

This function will create symmetric flow\+\_\+label value based on the local and remote qpn values. this will allow both the requester and responder to calculate the same flow\+\_\+label for a given connection.

This helper function should be used by driver in case the upper layer provide a zero flow\+\_\+label value. This is to improve entropy of R\+D\+MA traffic in the network. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a5377afdee199ea557aeaecaaa07f53d3}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a5377afdee199ea557aeaecaaa07f53d3}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+af\+\_\+ib -\/ Check if the port of device has the capability Native Infiniband Address. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

Infini\+Band addressing uses a port\textquotesingle{}s G\+U\+ID + Subnet Prefix to make a default G\+ID. Ro\+CE uses a different mechanism, but still generates a G\+ID via a prescribed mechanism and port specific data.

Return\+: true if the port uses a G\+ID address to identify devices on the network. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_ae0772615e0aa63c4034f4ae93a72b1d3}\label{ib__verbs_8h_ae0772615e0aa63c4034f4ae93a72b1d3}}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}} rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+eth\+\_\+ah (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__device}{ib\+\_\+device}} $\ast$}]{device,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{port\+\_\+num }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+eth\+\_\+ah -\/ Check if the port of device has the capability Ethernet Address Handle. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

Ro\+CE is Infini\+Band over Ethernet, and it uses a well defined technique to fabricate G\+I\+Ds over Ethernet/\+IP specific addresses native to the port. Normally, packet headers are generated by the sending host adapter, but when sending connectionless datagrams, we must manually inject the proper headers for the fabric we are communicating over.

Return\+: true if we are running as a Ro\+CE port and must force the addition of a Global Route Header built from our Ethernet Address Handle into our header list for connectionless packets. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a1935ce7f0bd6d86609a4af46b83fbe67}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a1935ce7f0bd6d86609a4af46b83fbe67}} 
\index{ib\_verbs.h@{ib\_verbs.h}!rdma\_cap\_ib\_cm@{rdma\_cap\_ib\_cm}}
\index{rdma\_cap\_ib\_cm@{rdma\_cap\_ib\_cm}!ib\_verbs.h@{ib\_verbs.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{rdma\_cap\_ib\_cm()}{rdma\_cap\_ib\_cm()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}} rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+ib\+\_\+cm (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__device}{ib\+\_\+device}} $\ast$}]{device,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{port\+\_\+num }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+ib\+\_\+cm -\/ Check if the port of device has the capability Infiniband Communication Manager. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} Infini\+Band Communication Manager is one of many pre-\/defined General Service Agents (G\+SA) that are accessed via the General Service \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceInterface}{Interface}} (G\+SI). It\textquotesingle{}s role is to facilitate establishment of connections between nodes as well as other management related tasks for established connections.

Return\+: true if the port supports an IB CM (this does not guarantee that a CM is actually running however). \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a752aad0efbdbebd66395b06625facb77}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a752aad0efbdbebd66395b06625facb77}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+ib\+\_\+mad -\/ Check if the port of a device supports Infiniband Management Datagrams. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

Management Datagrams (M\+AD) are a required part of the Infini\+Band specification and are supported on all Infini\+Band devices. A slightly extended version are also supported on O\+PA interfaces.

Return\+: true if the port supports sending/receiving of M\+AD packets. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_ab1f8ecf5cbb843dfc4e9f606a1c6d820}\label{ib__verbs_8h_ab1f8ecf5cbb843dfc4e9f606a1c6d820}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+ib\+\_\+mcast -\/ Check if the port of device has the capability Infiniband Multicast. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

Infini\+Band multicast registration is more complex than normal I\+Pv4 or I\+Pv6 multicast registration. Each Host Channel Adapter must register with the Subnet Manager when it wishes to join a multicast group. It should do so only once regardless of how many queue pairs it subscribes to this group. And it should leave the group only after all queue pairs attached to the group have been detached.

Return\+: true if the port must undertake the additional adminstrative overhead of registering/unregistering with the SM and tracking of the total number of queue pairs attached to the multicast group. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
% FIG 61
\mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a26075539e3f3554cda72c90345f0dad7}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a26075539e3f3554cda72c90345f0dad7}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+ib\+\_\+sa -\/ Check if the port of device has the capability Infiniband Subnet Administration. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

An Infini\+Band Subnet Administration (SA) service is a pre-\/defined General Service Agent (G\+SA) provided by the Subnet Manager (SM). On Infini\+Band fabrics, devices should resolve routes to other hosts by contacting the SA to query the proper route.

Return\+: true if the port should act as a client to the fabric Subnet Administration interface. This does not imply that the SA service is running locally. Here is the caller graph for this function\+:
% FIG 62
\mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_adf75ce74b0c40dd6979404dae25e4e71}\label{ib__verbs_8h_adf75ce74b0c40dd6979404dae25e4e71}} 
\index{ib\_verbs.h@{ib\_verbs.h}!rdma\_cap\_ib\_smi@{rdma\_cap\_ib\_smi}}
\index{rdma\_cap\_ib\_smi@{rdma\_cap\_ib\_smi}!ib\_verbs.h@{ib\_verbs.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{rdma\_cap\_ib\_smi()}{rdma\_cap\_ib\_smi()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily static \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}} rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+ib\+\_\+smi (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{const struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structib__device}{ib\+\_\+device}} $\ast$}]{device,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{build_8c_a10e94b422ef0c20dcdec20d31a1f5049}{u32}}}]{port\+\_\+num }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [inline]}, {\ttfamily [static]}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+ib\+\_\+smi -\/ Check if the port of a device provides an Infiniband Subnet Management Agent (S\+MA) on the Subnet Management \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceInterface}{Interface}} (S\+MI). @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

Each Infini\+Band node is required to provide a Subnet Management Agent that the subnet manager can access. Prior to the fabric being fully configured by the subnet manager, the S\+MA is accessed via a well known interface called the Subnet Management \mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceInterface}{Interface}} (S\+MI). This interface uses directed route packets to communicate with the SM to get around the chicken and egg problem of the SM needing to know what\textquotesingle{}s on the fabric in order to configure the fabric, and needing to configure the fabric in order to send packets to the devices on the fabric. These directed route packets do not need the fabric fully configured in order to reach their destination. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} S\+MI is the only method allowed to send directed route packets on an Infini\+Band fabric.

Return\+: true if the port provides an S\+MI. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_ae0177bc0a706d22cbd92c584886f4b62}\label{ib__verbs_8h_ae0177bc0a706d22cbd92c584886f4b62}}

rdma\+\_\+cap\+\_\+opa\+\_\+mad -\/ Check if the port of device provides support for O\+PA Management Datagrams. @device\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classDevice}{Device}} to check @port\+\_\+num\+: Port number to check

Intel Omni\+Path devices extend and/or replace the Infini\+Band Management datagrams with their own versions. These O\+PA M\+A\+Ds share many but not all of the characteristics of Infini\+Band M\+A\+Ds.

O\+PA M\+A\+Ds differ in the following ways\+:

1) M\+A\+Ds are variable size up to 2K I\+B\+TA defined M\+A\+Ds remain fixed at 256 bytes 2) O\+PA S\+M\+Ps must carry valid P\+Keys 3) O\+PA S\+MP packets are a different format

Return\+: true if the port supports O\+PA M\+AD packet formats. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a3d74323779c2f32dbb0f396fe9a81df3}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a3d74323779c2f32dbb0f396fe9a81df3}}

rdma\+\_\+flow\+\_\+label\+\_\+to\+\_\+udp\+\_\+sport -\/ generate a Ro\+CE v2 U\+DP src port value based on the flow\+\_\+label

This function will convert the 20 bit flow\+\_\+label input to a valid Ro\+CE v2 U\+DP src port 14 bit value. All Ro\+CE V2 drivers should use this same convention. \mbox{\Hypertarget{ib__verbs_8h_a562adc3c9d768d9d6ab9ae3adc809d86}\label{ib__verbs_8h_a562adc3c9d768d9d6ab9ae3adc809d86}}
